url = "https://api.exa.ai/contents"

payload = {
    "ids": ["https://arxiv.org/pdf/2307.06435"],
    "urls": ["https://arxiv.org/pdf/2307.06435"],
    "compliance": "hipaa",
    "text": False,
    "highlights": False,
    "summary": {
        "query": "Main developments",
        "schema": {
            "$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#",
            "title": "Title",
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "Property 1": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Description"
                },
                "Property 2": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "enum": ["option 1", "option 2", "option 3"],
                    "description": "Description"
                }
            },
            "required": ["Property 1"]
        }
    },
    "extras": {
        "links": 1,
        "imageLinks": 1,
        "richImageLinks": 0,
        "richLinks": 0,
        "codeBlocks": 0
    },

    "livecrawl": "preferred",
    "livecrawlTimeout": 1000,
    "maxAgeHours": 24,
    "subpages": 1,
    "subpageTarget": "sources"
}

    "x-api-key": "&lt;api-key&gt;",
    "Content-Type": "application/json"
}

print(response.text)</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#FFFFFF;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div><div data-hidden="" data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" hidden="" id="base-ui-_R_76b4jddbsnlhjiuasnpfiutb_" tabindex="0" inert="" data-index="-1" class="w-full min-w-full max-w-full h-full max-h-full relative focus-visible:outline-hidden before:content-[''] before:absolute before:inset-0 before:z-10 before:pointer-events-none before:rounded-xt focus-visible:before:outline focus-visible:before:outline-1 focus-visible:before:-outline-offset-2 focus-visible:before:outline-primary dark:focus-visible:before:outline-primary-light data-hidden:hidden [&amp;_[data-component-part=code-block-root]]:rounded-xt!"><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px;font-variant-ligatures:none;height:100%;background-color:#FFFFFF;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E" data-component-part="code-block-root" class="w-0 min-w-full max-w-full h-full dark:bg-codeblock text-sm leading-6 transition-[height] duration-300 ease-in-out code-block-background **:ring-0 **:outline-0 **:focus:ring-0 **:focus:outline-0 rounded-xt bg-white"><div role="presentation" data-id="base-ui-_R_v6b4jddbsnlhjiuasnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] py-3.5 px-4 base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="min-w-full! h-full children:my-0! children:shadow-none! children:bg-transparent!"><div class="font-mono whitespace-pre flex-none h-full text-xs leading-[1.35rem]" data-component-part="code-group-tab-content"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes"><code>const options = {
  method: 'POST',
  headers: {'x-api-key': '&lt;api-key&gt;', 'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
  body: JSON.stringify({
    ids: ['https://arxiv.org/pdf/2307.06435'],
    urls: ['https://arxiv.org/pdf/2307.06435'],
    compliance: 'hipaa',
    text: false,
    highlights: false,
    summary: {
      query: 'Main developments',
      schema: {
        $schema: 'http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#',
        title: 'Title',
        type: 'object',
        properties: {
          'Property 1': {type: 'string', description: 'Description'},
          'Property 2': {
            type: 'string',
            enum: ['option 1', 'option 2', 'option 3'],
            description: 'Description'
          }
        },
        required: ['Property 1']
      }
    },
    extras: {links: 1, imageLinks: 1, richImageLinks: 0, richLinks: 0, codeBlocks: 0},
    context: true,
    livecrawl: 'preferred',
    livecrawlTimeout: 1000,
    maxAgeHours: 24,
    subpages: 1,
    subpageTarget: 'sources'
  })
};

fetch('https://api.exa.ai/contents', options)
  .then(res =&gt; res.json())
  .then(res =&gt; console.log(res))
  .catch(err =&gt; console.error(err));</code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true"
